> AGENT

`routes_legacy.py` defines plain `async def` functions — NOT decorated with `@router.get()` etc. They're just callable functions that `routers/v1.py` wraps by calling `await legacy_handlers.health()` etc.

The `router = APIRouter()` on line 71 is unused. The functions are not registered on any router. They were designed to be called by the v1 router wrapper, not to be a self-contained router.

This means the correct approach for `__init__.py` is different. The old `__init__.py` had `@app.get()` decorators directly on the app object. Since `routes_legacy.py` functions aren't decorated, I can't just `include_router`. 

The right approach: `__init__.py` should register routes by decorating the `routes_legacy` functions onto the `app`.

> TOOL

{
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/src/cohezion/api/__init__.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Cohezion API - FastAPI server exposing swarm and MCP tools.\n\nThin entry point: creates the app, registers routes from\n``routes_legacy``, and re-exports singletons for backward\ncompatibility (tests/conftest.py resets them via\n``cohezion.api._vae_trainer = None``).\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport logging\nimport os\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nfrom fastapi import FastAPI, Request\nfrom fastapi.middleware.cors import CORSMiddleware\nfrom fastapi.responses import JSONResponse, RedirectResponse\nfrom fastapi.staticfiles import StaticFiles\n\nfrom cohezion.security.rate_limiter import get_rate_limiter\n\n# Re-export singletons so ``cohezion.api._vae_trainer`` still works.\nfrom .singletons import (  # noqa: F401\n    _compute_coherence,\n    _get_rl_policy,\n    _get_vae,\n    _rl_policy,\n    _vae_trainer,\n    set_token_client,\n)\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_CORS_ORIGINS = os.environ.get(\n    \"COHEZION_CORS_ORIGINS\",\n    \"http://localhost:3000,http://localhost:8080\",\n).split(\",\")\n\napp = FastAPI(\n    title=\"Cohezion API\",\n    description=\"AI Research Lab API - Swarm workflows and MCP tools\",\n    version=\"0.1.0\",\n    docs_url=\"/docs\" if os.environ.get(\"COHEZION_ENV\") != \"production\" else None,\n    redoc_url=\"/redoc\" if os.environ.get(\"COHEZION_ENV\") != \"production\" else None,\n)\n\napp.add_middleware(\n    CORSMiddleware,\n    allow_origins=_CORS_ORIGINS,\n    allow_credentials=False,\n    allow_methods=[\"GET\", \"POST\"],\n    allow_headers=[\"Content-Type\", \"Authorization\", \"X-Agent-Token\"],\n)\n\n\n@app.middleware(\"http\")\nasync def rate_limit_middleware(request: Request, call_next):\n    limiter = get_rate_limiter()\n    client_ip = request.client.host if request.client else \"unknown\"\n    result = limiter.check(client_ip, request.url.path)\n    if not result.allowed:\n        return JSONResponse(\n            status_code=429,\n            headers={\n                \"Retry-After\": str(int(result.reset_after) + 1),\n                \"X-RateLimit-Limit\": str(result.limit),\n                \"X-RateLimit-Remaining\": \"0\",\n            },\n            content={\"detail\": \"Rate limit exceeded\"},\n        )\n    response = await call_next(request)\n    response.headers[\"X-RateLimit-Remaining\"] = str(result.remaining)\n    return response\n\n\nstatic_dir = Path(__file__).parent / \"static\"\nif static_dir.exists():\n    app.mount(\"/static\", StaticFiles(directory=static_dir, html=True), name=\"static\")\n\n\n@app.get(\"/\")\nasync def root():\n    return RedirectResponse(url=\"/static/index.html\")\n\n\ndef _register_routes() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Bind all route handlers from routes_legacy onto the app.\n\n    This avoids duplicating route definitions here. The handlers\n    in ``routes_legacy`` are plain async functions; this function\n    attaches them to the FastAPI ``app`` with the correct HTTP\n    method and path.\n    \"\"\"\n    from . import routes_legacy as h\n    from .models import (\n        CompoundExecuteResponse,\n        CompoundFeedbackResponse,\n        CompoundHealthResponse,\n        CompoundHistoryResponse,\n        CompoundMetricsResponse,\n        DebateResponse,\n        FlumeDecodeResponse,\n        FlumeEncodeResponse,\n        FlumeInterpolateResponse,\n        FlumeStatusResponse,\n        FlumeTrainResponse,\n        KnowledgeQueryResponse,\n        PipelineStatusResponse,\n        RlEpisodeResponse,\n        RlPolicyInfoResponse,\n        RLPolicyResponse,\n        RlStepResponse,\n        RLTrainResponse,\n        SkillExecuteResponse,\n        SwarmExecuteResponse,\n        SystemMetricsResponse,\n        TemplateParseResponse,\n        TokenMetricsResponse,\n        TrainingMetricsResponse,\n    )\n\n    # fmt: off\n    # System\n    app.get(\"/health\")(h.health)\n    # MCP\n    app.get(\"/mcp/servers\")(h.list_servers)\n    app.get(\"/mcp/tools\")(h.list_tools)\n    # Knowledge\n    app.post(\"/knowledge/search\")(h.search_knowledge)\n    app.get(\"/knowledge/skills\")(h.list_skills)\n    app.get(\"/knowledge/skills/{skill_name}\")(h.get_skill)\n    # Swarm\n    app.post(\"/swarm/debate\", response_model=DebateResponse)(h.run_debate)\n    app.get(\"/swarm/perspectives\")(h.get_perspectives)\n    app.get(\"/swarm/metrics\")(h.get_metrics)\n    # Notebooks\n    app.get(\"/notebooks\")(h.list_notebooks)\n    app.get(\"/notebooks/{name}\")(h.get_notebook)\n    # Simulations\n    app.get(\"/simulations\")(h.list_simulations)\n    app.get(\"/simulations/{sim_id}\")(h.get_simulation)\n    # Journeys\n    app.get(\"/journeys\")(h.list_journeys)\n    app.get(\"/journeys/{journey_id}\")(h.get_journey)\n    app.get(\"/journeys/{journey_id}/trajectory\")(h.get_journey_trajectory)\n    app.post(\"/journeys/demo\")(h.create_demo_journey)\n    app.get(\"/journeys/{journey_id}/visualize\")(h.visualize_journey)\n    app.get(\"/journeys/{journey_id}/plot\")(h.plot_journey)\n    app.get(\"/compare/calm-vs-llm/{journey_id}\")(h.compare_calm_llm)\n    # FLUME VAE\n    app.post(\"/flume/train\", response_model=FlumeTrainResponse)(h.train_flume)\n    app.get(\"/flume/status\", response_model=FlumeStatusResponse)(h.flume_status)\n    app.post(\"/flume/encode\", response_model=FlumeEncodeResponse)(h.flume_encode)\n    app.post(\"/flume/decode\", response_model=FlumeDecodeResponse)(h.flume_decode)\n    app.post(\"/flume/interpolate\", response_model=FlumeInterpolateResponse)(h.flume_interpolate)\n    # Templates\n    app.post(\"/templates/parse\", response_model=TemplateParseResponse)(h.parse_template)\n    # RL\n    app.post(\"/rl/train\", response_model=RLTrainResponse)(h.train_rl)\n    app.get(\"/rl/policy/{agent_id}\", response_model=RLPolicyResponse)(h.get_rl_policy)\n    app.post(\"/rl/step\", response_model=RlStepResponse)(h.rl_step)\n    app.post(\"/rl/episode\", response_model=RlEpisodeResponse)(h.rl_episode)\n    app.get(\"/rl/policy-info\", response_model=RlPolicyInfoResponse)(h.rl_policy_info)\n    # Skills\n    app.post(\"/skills/{skill_name}/execute\", response_model=SkillExecuteResponse)(h.execute_skill)\n    app.post(\"/query/find-capable-agent\")(h.find_capable_agent)\n    app.get(\"/skills/list\")(h.list_prime_skills)\n    # Metrics\n    app.get(\"/metrics/agents\")(h.metrics_agents)\n    app.get(\"/metrics/training\", response_model=TrainingMetricsResponse)(h.metrics_training)\n    app.get(\"/metrics/pipeline\", response_model=PipelineStatusResponse)(h.metrics_pipeline)\n    app.get(\"/metrics/system\", response_model=SystemMetricsResponse)(h.metrics_system)\n    app.post(\"/knowledge/query\", response_model=KnowledgeQueryResponse)(h.knowledge_query)\n    app.get(\"/metrics/tokens\", response_model=TokenMetricsResponse)(h.metrics_tokens)\n    # Swarm execution\n    app.post(\"/swarm/execute\", response_model=SwarmExecuteResponse)(h.swarm_execute)\n    # Compound engineering\n    app.get(\"/metrics/compound\", response_model=CompoundMetricsResponse)(h.metrics_compound)\n    app.post(\"/compound/execute\", response_model=CompoundExecuteResponse)(h.compound_execute)\n    app.post(\"/compound/feedback\", response_model=CompoundFeedbackResponse)(h.compound_feedback)\n    app.get(\"/compound/health\", response_model=CompoundHealthResponse)(h.compound_health)\n    app.get(\"/compound/history/{skill_name}\", response_model=CompoundHistoryResponse)(h.compound_history)\n    # fmt: on\n\n\n_register_routes()\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    import uvicorn\n\n    uvicorn.run(app, host=\"0.0.0.0\", port=8080)\n"
}

> AGENT

As suspected — setting `api_module._vae_trainer = None` doesn't propagate to `singletons._vae_trainer`. The `_get_vae()` function in `singletons.py` reads its own module global, not the re-exported one. This means conftest's reset won't actually clear the singleton.

I need to fix this. The cleanest solution: make `_get_vae()` and `_get_rl_policy()` check and set the state via a mutable container, or make conftest reset the singletons module directly. But the safest approach (minimal change to conftest, maximum backward compat) is to make `__init__.py` handle the reset by proxying to singletons.

Actually, the simplest fix: update conftest to reset `cohezion.api.singletons._vae_trainer` instead of `cohezion.api._vae_trainer`. But that changes conftest behavior. 

Better approach: make `__init__.py` use properties or a `__setattr__` hook. But that's complex for a module.

Simplest correct fix: don't re-export the bare variables. Instead, have `__init__.py` use `__getattr__` and `__setattr__` to proxy to singletons.

  "new_string": "# Re-export callable singletons (these are functions, not mutable state).\nfrom .singletons import (  # noqa: F401\n    _compute_coherence,\n    _get_rl_policy,\n    _get_vae,\n    set_token_client,\n)\n\n# Proxy mutable singleton variables (_vae_trainer, _rl_policy) so that\n# ``cohezion.api._vae_trainer = None`` propagates to the singletons module\n# (required by tests/conftest.py and mycelium/synthesizer.py).\nfrom . import singletons as _singletons\n\n_PROXIED_ATTRS = {\"_vae_trainer\", \"_rl_policy\"}\n\n\ndef __getattr__(name: str):\n    if name in _PROXIED_ATTRS:\n        return getattr(_singletons, name)\n    raise AttributeError(f\"module 'cohezion.api' has no attribute {name!r}\")\n\n\ndef __setattr__(name: str, value):  # type: ignore[override]\n    if name in _PROXIED_ATTRS:\n        setattr(_singletons, name, value)\n        return\n    # Fall through to normal module attribute setting\n    globals()[name] = value\n\n\nlogging.basicConfig(level=logging.INFO)"
}

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/home/mike-anderson/dev/cohezion/src/cohezion/api/__init__.py",
  "old_string": "# Proxy mutable singleton variables (_vae_trainer, _rl_policy) so that\n# ``cohezion.api._vae_trainer = None`` propagates to the singletons module\n# (required by tests/conftest.py and mycelium/synthesizer.py).\nfrom . import singletons as _singletons\n\n_PROXIED_ATTRS = {\"_vae_trainer\", \"_rl_policy\"}\n\n\ndef __getattr__(name: str):\n    if name in _PROXIED_ATTRS:\n        return getattr(_singletons, name)\n    raise AttributeError(f\"module 'cohezion.api' has no attribute {name!r}\")\n\n\ndef __setattr__(name: str, value):  # type: ignore[override]\n    if name in _PROXIED_ATTRS:\n        setattr(_singletons, name, value)\n        return\n    # Fall through to normal module attribute setting\n    globals()[name] = value",
  "new_string": "# Proxy mutable singleton variables (_vae_trainer, _rl_policy) so that\n# ``cohezion.api._vae_trainer = None`` propagates to the singletons module\n# (required by tests/conftest.py and mycelium/synthesizer.py).\nimport sys\nimport types\n\nfrom . import singletons as _singletons\n\n_PROXIED_ATTRS = frozenset({\"_vae_trainer\", \"_rl_policy\"})\n\n\nclass _ApiModule(types.ModuleType):\n    \"\"\"Custom module class that proxies singleton reads/writes to singletons.py.\"\"\"\n\n    def __getattr__(self, name: str):\n        if name in _PROXIED_ATTRS:\n            return getattr(_singletons, name)\n        raise AttributeError(f\"module 'cohezion.api' has no attribute {name!r}\")\n\n    def __setattr__(self, name: str, value):\n        if name in _PROXIED_ATTRS:\n            setattr(_singletons, name, value)\n            return\n        super().__setattr__(name, value)\n\n\nsys.modules[__name__].__class__ = _ApiModule"
}

    40→
    41→def _run_async_guardrail(coro: Any) -> Any:
    42→    """Execute async guardrail check in sync context.
    43→
    44→    Non-blocking on failure - logs and returns None.
    45→
    46→    Args:
    47→        coro: Async coroutine to execute
    48→
    49→    Returns:
    50→        Result of coroutine or None on failure
    51→    """
    52→    try:
    53→        return asyncio.run(coro)
    54→    except Exception as e:
    55→        logger.debug(f"Guardrail check failed (non-blocking): {e}")
    56→        return None
    57→
    58→
    59→@dataclass
    60→class ExecutionResult:
    61→    """Result of a compound execution."""
    62→
    63→    success: bool
    64→    output: str
    65→    metrics: dict[str, Any]
    66→    duration_seconds: float
    67→    vault_experiment_path: str = ""
    68→    vault_decision_paths: list[str] | None = None
    69→    token_metrics: dict[str, Any] | None = None
    70→
    71→
    72→class CompoundExecutor:
    73→    """Executor for compound engineering tasks with vault integration.
    74→
    75→    Lifecycle:
    76→      1. get_experience_guidance() - Query vault for similar tasks
    77→      2. execute_task() - Run the task with token-efficient client
    78→      3. Logs are persisted to vault automatically
    79→      4. extract_patterns() - Save reusable insights
    80→

   909→    def _compute_token_delta(
   910→        self,
   911→        metrics_before: dict[str, Any] | None,
   912→        metrics_after: dict[str, Any],
   913→    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
   914→        """Compute token metric deltas (tokens used in this execution).
   915→
   916→        Args:
   917→            metrics_before: Token metrics before execution
   918→            metrics_after: Token metrics after execution
   919→
   920→        Returns:
   921→            Dict with token delta information
   922→        """
   923→        if not metrics_before:
   924→            # First execution, can't compute delta
   925→            return metrics_after
   926→
   927→        delta: dict[str, Any] = {}
   928→
   929→        # Compute differences
   930→        if "total_tokens" in metrics_after and "total_tokens" in metrics_before:
   931→            delta["tokens_used"] = metrics_after["total_tokens"] - metrics_before["total_tokens"]
   932→        if "api_calls" in metrics_after and "api_calls" in metrics_before:
   933→            delta["api_calls_made"] = metrics_after["api_calls"] - metrics_before["api_calls"]
   934→        if "cache_hits" in metrics_after and "cache_hits" in metrics_before:
   935→            delta["cache_hits"] = metrics_after["cache_hits"] - metrics_before["cache_hits"]
   936→        if "cache_misses" in metrics_after and "cache_misses" in metrics_before:
   937→            delta["cache_misses"] = metrics_after["cache_misses"] - metrics_before["cache_misses"]
   938→
   939→        # Include final hit rate
   940→        if "cache_hit_rate" in metrics_after:
   941→            delta["cache_hit_rate"] = metrics_after["cache_hit_rate"]
   942→
   943→        # Pass through model name and other non-delta fields
   944→        if "model" in metrics_after:
   945→            delta["model"] = metrics_after["model"]
   946→        if "combined_hit_rate" in metrics_after:
   947→            delta["combined_hit_rate"] = metrics_after["combined_hit_rate"]
   948→        if "tokens_per_second" in metrics_after:
   949→            delta["tokens_per_second"] = metrics_after["tokens_per_second"]
   950→
   951→        return delta

cohezion.security.guardrail_pipeline import GuardrailPipeline\n\nfrom .executor import CompoundExecutor\n\n\nclass ExecutorFactory:\n    \"\"\"Factory for creating compound executors with vault integration.\"\"\"\n\n    _instance: CompoundExecutor | None = None\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def create(\n        mcp_client: MCPClient,\n        token_client: Any | None = None,\n        guardrail_pipeline: GuardrailPipeline | None = None,\n        enable_guardrails: bool = True,\n        inflection_detector: Any | None = None,\n        skill_refiner: Any | None = None,\n        enable_skill_refinement: bool = True,\n        metrics_collector: Any | None = None,\n        journey_tracker: Any | None = None,\n        journey_persistence: Any | None = None,\n get_singleton(\n        mcp_client: MCPClient,\n        token_client: Any | None = None,\n        guardrail_pipeline: GuardrailPipeline | None = None,\n        enable_guardrails: bool = True,\n        inflection_detector: Any | None = None,\n        skill_refiner: Any | None = None,\n        enable_skill_refinement: bool = True,\n        metrics_collector: Any | None = None,\n      alignment_analyzer=alignment_analyzer,\n                enable_alignment_analysis=enable_alignment_analysis,\n                degradation_detector=degradation_detector,\n                model_quality_classifier=model_quality_classifier,\n                retrospection_engine=retrospection_engine,\n                universe_bridge=universe_bridge,\n            )\n        return ExecutorFactory._instance\n\n    @staticmethod\n    def reset_singleton() -> None:\n        \"\"\"Reset singleton for testing.\"\"\"\n        ExecutorFactory._instance = None\n"

  "content": "\"\"\"Post-execution pipeline steps for CompoundExecutor.\n\nExtracted from the monolithic ``execute_task`` method. Each function\nperforms one non-blocking pipeline step, returning any vault paths\ncreated. All steps are wrapped in try/except to be non-blocking.\n\"\"\"\n\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport asyncio\nimport json\nimport logging\nimport time\nfrom typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any\n\nif TYPE_CHECKING:\n    from .executor import CompoundExecutor, ExecutionResult\n\nlogger = logging.getLogger(__name__)\n\n_background_tasks: set[asyncio.Task[Any]] = set()\n\n\ndef run_anomaly_detection(\n    executor: CompoundExecutor,\n    result: ExecutionResult,\n    skill_name: str,\n    task_description: str,\n    project: str,\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Step 5: Detect anomalies and log critical inflection points.\"\"\"\n    paths: list[str] = []\n    try:\n        from cohezion.compound.inflection_detector import Severity\n\n        anomaly = executor.inflection_detector.detect_anomaly(result)\n        result.metrics[\"anomaly_severity\"] = anomaly.severity.value\n        result.metrics[\"anomaly_score\"] = anomaly.score\n        logger.debug(\n            \"Anomaly detection: severity=%s, score=%.2f\",\n            anomaly.severity.value,\n            anomaly.score,\n        )\n        if anomaly.severity == Severity.CRITICAL:\n            logger.warning(\"Critical inflection point: %d issues\", len(anomaly.issues))\n            try:\n                dp = executor.log_inflection_point(\n                    title=f\"Critical anomaly in {skill_name}\",\n                    context=f\"Task: {task_description}\\nIssues: {'; '.join(anomaly.issues)}\",\n                    decision=\"Re-execution recommended\",\n                    rationale=(\n                        f\"Quality score {anomaly.score:.2f}, \"\n                        f\"{anomaly.recommendations[0] if anomaly.recommendations else 'Investigate issues'}\"\n                    ),\n                    project=project,\n                )\n                if dp:\n                    paths.append(dp)\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.debug(\"Failed to log inflection point: %s\", e)\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Anomaly detection failed (non-blocking): %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n    return paths\n\n\ndef run_alignment_analysis(\n    executor: CompoundExecutor,\n    result: ExecutionResult,\n    parsed_request: Any,\n    operation_type: str,\n    project: str,\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Step 5.5: Analyze request-execution alignment.\"\"\"\n    paths: list[str] = []\n    if not (executor._enable_alignment_analysis and executor.alignment_analyzer and parsed_request):\n        return paths\n    try:\n        from cohezion.compound.inflection_detector import AnomalyDetection, Severity\n\n        anomaly_analysis = None\n        if \"anomaly_severity\" in result.metrics:\n            severity_enum = Severity(result.metrics.get(\"anomaly_severity\", \"info\"))\n            anomaly_analysis = AnomalyDetection(\n                severity=severity_enum,\n                score=result.metrics.get(\"anomaly_score\", 0.0),\n                issues=result.metrics.get(\"anomaly_issues\", []),\n                recommendations=result.metrics.get(\"anomaly_recommendations\", []),\n                should_reexecute=False,\n            )\n        alignment = executor.alignment_analyzer.analyze_alignment(\n            parsed_request, result, operation_type, anomaly_analysis\n        )\n        if alignment.misalignment_score > 0.3:\n            vault_path = executor.alignment_analyzer.log_alignment_to_vault(\n                parsed_request, alignment, project\n            )\n            if vault_path:\n                paths.append(vault_path)\n        result.metrics[\"alignment\"] = {\n            \"misalignment_score\": alignment.misalignment_score,\n            \"intent_match\": alignment.intent_match_score,\n            \"constraint_satisfaction\": alignment.constraint_satisfaction,\n            \"criteria_satisfaction\": alignment.criteria_satisfaction,\n            \"violations_count\": len(alignment.violations),\n            \"failures_count\": len(alignment.failures),\n            \"issues_count\": len(alignment.issues),\n            \"should_retry\": alignment.should_retry,\n        }\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Alignment analysis failed (non-blocking): %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n    return paths\n\n\ndef run_pattern_extraction(\n    executor: CompoundExecutor,\n    experiment_path: str,\n    skill_name: str,\n    operation_type: str,\n    task_description: str,\n    metrics: dict[str, Any],\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Step 6: Extract patterns from successful execution.\"\"\"\n    paths: list[str] = []\n    try:\n        pattern_path = executor.logger.extract_execution_pattern(\n            source_path=experiment_path,\n            pattern_name=f\"{skill_name}_{operation_type}_success\",\n            description=(\n                f\"Successful execution pattern for {skill_name} \"\n                f\"operation: {operation_type}. Task: {task_description[:100]}\"\n            ),\n            code_example=f\"Result metrics: {json.dumps(metrics, indent=2)}\",\n            domain=\"compound-engineering\",\n        )\n        if pattern_path:\n            paths.append(pattern_path)\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.warning(\"Failed to extract pattern: %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n    return paths\n\n\ndef run_retrospection(\n    executor: CompoundExecutor,\n    result: ExecutionResult,\n    skill_name: str,\n) -> tuple[bool, dict[str, Any] | None]:\n    \"\"\"Step 7.3: Run retrospection analysis (gates skill refinement).\n\n    Returns:\n        (should_refine, retrospection_context)\n    \"\"\"\n    if not executor._retrospection_engine:\n        return True, None\n    try:\n        ctx = executor._retrospection_engine.analyze_execution_result(result, skill_name)\n        should_refine = ctx.get(\"should_refine\", True)\n        if ctx.get(\"insights\"):\n            result.metrics[\"retrospection_insights\"] = ctx[\"insights\"]\n        logger.debug(\n            \"Retrospection: should_refine=%s, compound=%.3f\",\n            should_refine,\n            ctx.get(\"compound_score\", 0.0),\n        )\n        return should_refine, ctx\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Retrospection failed (non-blocking): %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n        return True, None\n\n\ndef run_skill_refinement(\n    executor: CompoundExecutor,\n    result: ExecutionResult,\n    skill_name: str,\n    operation_type: str,\n    decision_paths: list[str],\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Step 7: Refine skills based on execution results.\"\"\"\n    paths: list[str] = []\n    if not executor.skill_refiner:\n        return paths\n    try:\n        exec_result = {\n            \"success\": result.success,\n            \"output\": result.output,\n            \"metrics\": result.metrics,\n            \"duration_seconds\": result.duration_seconds,\n            \"token_metrics\": result.token_metrics,\n        }\n        refined_path = executor.skill_refiner.refine(\n            skill_name=skill_name,\n            operation_type=operation_type,\n            execution_result=exec_result,\n            patterns_extracted=decision_paths,\n        )\n        if refined_path:\n            logger.info(\"Skill refined: %s\", refined_path)\n            paths.append(refined_path)\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Skill refinement failed (non-blocking): %s\", e, exc_info=True)\n    return paths\n\n\ndef run_degradation_check(\n    executor: CompoundExecutor,\n    result: ExecutionResult,\n    task_description: str,\n    project: str,\n) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Step 7.5: Check for degradation and manage HIHO band.\"\"\"\n    paths: list[str] = []\n    coherence_val = result.metrics.get(\"coherence\", 0.5)\n\n    if 0.4 <= coherence_val <= 0.6 and executor._degradation_mode:\n        logger.info(\"Cohesion returned to HIHO band (%.2f), exiting degradation mode\", coherence_val)\n        executor._degradation_mode = False\n\n    if not executor._degradation_detector:\n        return paths\n    try:\n        degradation_metrics = {\n            \"combined_hit_rate\": 0.0,\n            \"tokens_per_second\": 0.0,\n            \"mean_coherence\": coherence_val,\n            \"elapsed_seconds\": result.duration_seconds,\n            \"success_rate\": 1.0 if result.success else 0.0,\n        }\n        if result.token_metrics:\n            degradation_metrics[\"combined_hit_rate\"] = result.token_metrics.get(\n                \"cache_hit_rate\", result.token_metrics.get(\"combined_hit_rate\", 0.0)\n            )\n            degradation_metrics[\"tokens_per_second\"] = result.token_metrics.get(\"tokens_per_second\", 0.0)\n        alerts = executor._degradation_detector.check_degradation(degradation_metrics)\n        if alerts:\n            result.metrics[\"degradation_alerts\"] = len(alerts)\n            for alert in alerts:\n                logger.warning(\"Degradation alert [%s]: %s\", alert.severity.value, alert.message)\n            critical_alerts = [a for a in alerts if a.severity.value == \"CRITICAL\"]\n            if critical_alerts:\n                executor._degradation_mode = True\n                result.metrics[\"execution_degraded\"] = True\n            for alert in critical_alerts:\n                try:\n                    dp = executor.log_inflection_point(\n                        title=f\"Degradation: {alert.metric}\",\n                        context=f\"Task: {task_description}\\n{alert.message}\",\n                        decision=\"Investigate degradation\",\n                        rationale=(\n                            f\"Current: {alert.current_value:.3f}, \"\n                            f\"Baseline: {alert.baseline_value:.3f}, \"\n                            f\"Threshold: {alert.threshold:.3f}\"\n                        ),\n                        project=project,\n                    )\n                    if dp:\n                        paths.append(dp)\n                except Exception as e:\n                    logger.debug(\"Failed to log degradation alert: %s\", e)\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Degradation detection failed (non-blocking): %s\", e)\n    return paths\n\n\ndef run_quality_recording(executor: CompoundExecutor, result: ExecutionResult) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Step 7.7: Record model quality.\"\"\"\n    if not executor._model_quality_classifier:\n        return\n    try:\n        model_name = \"unknown\"\n        tokens_used = 0\n        if result.token_metrics:\n            model_name = result.token_metrics.get(\"model\", \"unknown\")\n            tokens_used = result.token_metrics.get(\"tokens_used\", 0)\n        executor._model_quality_classifier.add_execution(\n            model=model_name,\n            coherence=result.metrics.get(\"coherence\", 0.5),\n            success=result.success,\n            tokens_used=tokens_used,\n            duration=result.duration_seconds,\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Model quality recording failed (non-blocking): %s\", e)\n\n\ndef run_metrics_recording(executor: CompoundExecutor, result: ExecutionResult, skill_name: str) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Step 8: Record execution metrics.\"\"\"\n    if not executor._metrics_collector:\n        return\n    try:\n        tokens_used = 0\n        model_used = \"\"\n        if result.token_metrics:\n            tokens_used = result.token_metrics.get(\"tokens_used\", 0)\n            model_used = result.token_metrics.get(\"model\", \"\")\n        executor._metrics_collector.record_execution(\n            skill_name=skill_name,\n            success=result.success,\n            tokens_used=tokens_used,\n            duration_ms=result.duration_seconds * 1000,\n            model_used=model_used,\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Metrics recording failed (non-blocking): %s\", e)\n\n\ndef run_journey_tracking(\n    executor: CompoundExecutor,\n    result: ExecutionResult,\n    task_description: str,\n    operation_type: str,\n) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Step 9: Track journey. Returns True if a point was tracked.\"\"\"\n    if not executor._journey_tracker:\n        return False\n    try:\n        point = executor._journey_tracker.track_execution(result, task_description, operation_type)\n        if point and point.metadata:\n            result.metrics[\"phi_score\"] = point.metadata.get(\"phi_score\", 0.0)\n        if executor._journey_persistence and point:\n            try:\n                point_data = {\n                    \"coherence\": point.coherence,\n                    \"efficiency\": point.efficiency,\n                    \"operation_type\": point.operation_type,\n                    \"task_description\": point.task_description[:200],\n                    \"timestamp\": point.timestamp,\n                }\n                if point.metadata:\n                    point_data[\"metadata\"] = point.metadata\n                exec_id = f\"exec_{int(time.time())}\"\n                try:\n                    asyncio.get_running_loop()\n                    task = asyncio.ensure_future(\n                        executor._journey_persistence.save_trajectory_point(exec_id, point_data)\n                    )\n                    _background_tasks.add(task)\n                    task.add_done_callback(_background_tasks.discard)\n                except RuntimeError:\n                    asyncio.run(executor._journey_persistence.save_trajectory_point(exec_id, point_data))\n            except Exception as e:\n                logger.debug(\"Journey persistence failed (non-blocking): %s\", e)\n        return True\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Journey tracking failed (non-blocking): %s\", e)\n        return False\n\n\ndef run_universe_bridge(\n    executor: CompoundExecutor,\n    result: ExecutionResult,\n    universe_journey_id: str | None,\n    operation_type: str,\n    journey_tracked: bool,\n) -> None:\n    \"\"\"Steps 9.5 and 10: Update universe bridge with trajectory and completion.\"\"\"\n    if not executor._universe_bridge or not universe_journey_id:\n        return\n    # Step 9.5: Add trajectory point\n    if journey_tracked and executor._journey_tracker:\n        try:\n            last_point = executor._journey_tracker.get_last_point()\n            if last_point:\n                executor._universe_bridge.add_point(\n                    universe_journey_id,\n                    last_point,\n                    step_number=executor._journey_tracker.get_recent_point_count(),\n                    action=operation_type,\n                )\n        except Exception as e:\n            logger.debug(\"Universe bridge point failed (non-blocking): %s\", e)\n    # Step 10: Complete journey\n    try:\n        phi = result.metrics.get(\"phi_score\", 0.0)\n        executor._universe_bridge.complete_journey(\n            universe_journey_id,\n            success=result.success,\n            phi_score=phi,\n            output=result.output[:500],\n        )\n    except Exception as e:\n        logger.debug(\"Universe bridge completion failed (non-blocking): %s\", e)\n"
}
